   1: "use client";
   2: 
   3: import { useState } from "react";
   4: import { App, Button, Form, Input, Typography } from "antd";
   5: 
   6: import { buildAuthRedirectUrl } from "@/lib/auth/redirect-url";
   7: import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase/browser";
   8: 
   9: const { Paragraph, Title } = Typography;
  10: 
  11: type Fields = { email: string };
  12: 
  13: export function PasswordResetRequestForm() {
  14:   const { message } = App.useApp();
  15:   const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  16:   const [sentTo, setSentTo] = useState<string | null>(null);
  17: 
  18:   async function handleSubmit(values: Fields) {
  19:     setSubmitting(true);
  20:     const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();
  21:     const { error } = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(values.email, {
  22:       redirectTo: buildAuthRedirectUrl("/password-reset/confirm"),
  23:     });
  24:     setSubmitting(false);
  25:     if (error) {
  26:       message.error(`전송 실패: ${error.message}`);
  27:       return;
  28:     }
  29:     setSentTo(values.email);
  30:   }
  31: 
  32:   if (sentTo) {
  33:     return (
  34:       <div>
  35:         <Title level={3}>이메일을 확인하세요</Title>
  36:         <Paragraph>
  37:           <strong>{sentTo}</strong> 로 비밀번호 재설정 링크를 보냈습니다. 메일
  38:           안의 링크를 누르면 새 비밀번호 설정 화면으로 이동합니다.
  39:         </Paragraph>
  40:       </div>
  41:     );
  42:   }
  43: 
  44:   return (
  45:     <Form
  46:       layout="vertical"
  47:       onFinish={handleSubmit}
  48:       requiredMark={false}
  49:     >
  50:       <Paragraph>
  51:         가입하신 이메일을 입력하시면 비밀번호 재설정 링크를 보내드립니다.
  52:       </Paragraph>
  53:       <Form.Item
  54:         label="이메일"
  55:         name="email"
  56:         rules={[
  57:           { required: true, message: "이메일을 입력하세요" },
  58:           { type: "email", message: "올바른 이메일 형식이 아닙니다" },
  59:         ]}
  60:       >
  61:         <Input autoComplete="email" />
  62:       </Form.Item>
  63:       <Form.Item>
  64:         <Button type="primary" htmlType="submit" block loading={submitting}>
  65:           재설정 링크 보내기
  66:         </Button>
  67:       </Form.Item>
  68:     </Form>
  69:   );
  70: }
FILE src/lib/auth/redirect-url.ts
   1: // Phase 7-B (Plan rev3 Task 1, R-10 mitigation).
   2: //
   3: // Single builder for all Supabase Auth redirects (signUp emailRedirectTo,
   4: // signInWithOtp emailRedirectTo, resetPasswordForEmail redirectTo).
   5: // Always returns an absolute http(s) URL; never a bare relative path.
   6: 
   7: const DEV_FALLBACK = "http://127.0.0.1:3000";
   8: 
   9: function ensureLeadingSlash(path: string): string {
  10:   return path.startsWith("/") ? path : `/${path}`;
  11: }
  12: 
  13: function stripTrailingSlash(url: string): string {
  14:   return url.endsWith("/") ? url.slice(0, -1) : url;
  15: }
  16: 
  17: function resolveSiteUrl(): string {
  18:   const env = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL?.trim();
  19:   if (env && env.length > 0) {
  20:     // Validate scheme — reject javascript:, data:, etc.
  21:     if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(env)) {
  22:       throw new Error(
  23:         `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL must start with http:// or https://, got: ${env}`,
  24:       );
  25:     }
  26:     return stripTrailingSlash(env);
  27:   }
  28: 
  29:   if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "development") {
  30:     return DEV_FALLBACK;
  31:   }
  32: 
  33:   throw new Error(
  34:     "NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL is required in non-development environments",
  35:   );
  36: }
  37: 
  38: export function buildAuthRedirectUrl(path: string): string {
  39:   const site = resolveSiteUrl();
  40:   return `${site}${ensureLeadingSlash(path)}`;
  41: }
FILE src/components/auth/SignUpForm.tsx
   1: "use client";
   2: 
   3: // Phase 7-B (original) + Phase 8-D (verify-email redirect)
   4: // - After successful sign-up, router.push('/auth/verify-email?email=...')
   5: //   instead of in-place "이메일 확인하세요" state. Verify page handles
   6: //   resend with 60s cooldown and survives reloads/deep-links.
   7: 
   8: import { useState } from "react";
   9: import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
  10: import { App, Button, Checkbox, Form, Input, Typography } from "antd";
  11: 
  12: import { buildAuthRedirectUrl } from "@/lib/auth/redirect-url";
  13: import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase/browser";
  14: 
  15: const { Paragraph, Title } = Typography;
  16: 
  17: type SignUpFields = {
  18:   email: string;
  19:   password: string;
  20:   passwordConfirm: string;
  21:   displayName?: string;
  22:   terms: boolean;
  23: };
  24: 
  25: export function SignUpForm() {
  26:   const { message } = App.useApp();
  27:   const router = useRouter();
  28:   const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  29:   const [form] = Form.useForm<SignUpFields>();
  30: 
  31:   async function handleSignUp(values: SignUpFields) {
  32:     setSubmitting(true);
  33:     const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();
  34:     const { error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({
  35:       email: values.email,
  36:       password: values.password,
  37:       options: {
  38:         data: values.displayName ? { display_name: values.displayName } : undefined,
  39:         emailRedirectTo: buildAuthRedirectUrl(
  40:           "/auth/callback?next=/onboarding/learning-goal",
  41:         ),
  42:       },
  43:     });
  44: 
  45:     if (error) {
  46:       setSubmitting(false);
  47:       message.error(`가입 실패: ${error.message}`);
  48:       return;
  49:     }
  50:     router.push(`/auth/verify-email?email=${encodeURIComponent(values.email)}`);
  51:   }
  52: 
  53:   return (
  54:     <Form
  55:       form={form}
  56:       layout="vertical"
  57:       onFinish={handleSignUp}
  58:       requiredMark={false}
  59:     >
  60:       <Form.Item
  61:         label="이메일"
  62:         name="email"
  63:         rules={[
  64:           { required: true, message: "이메일을 입력하세요" },
  65:           { type: "email", message: "올바른 이메일 형식이 아닙니다" },
  66:         ]}
  67:       >
  68:         <Input autoComplete="email" placeholder="you@example.com" />
  69:       </Form.Item>
  70: 
  71:       <Form.Item

FILE src/components/auth/PasswordResetConfirmForm.tsx
   1: "use client";
   2: 
   3: import { useState } from "react";
   4: import { App, Button, Form, Input, Typography } from "antd";
   5: import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
   6: 
   7: import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase/browser";
   8: 
   9: const { Paragraph, Title } = Typography;
  10: 
  11: type Fields = { password: string; passwordConfirm: string };
  12: 
  13: export function PasswordResetConfirmForm() {
  14:   const { message } = App.useApp();
  15:   const router = useRouter();
  16:   const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  17: 
  18:   async function handleSubmit(values: Fields) {
  19:     setSubmitting(true);
  20:     const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();
  21:     const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({
  22:       password: values.password,
  23:     });
  24:     setSubmitting(false);
  25:     if (error) {
  26:       message.error(`비밀번호 변경 실패: ${error.message}`);
  27:       return;
  28:     }
  29:     message.success("비밀번호가 변경되었습니다. 다시 로그인하세요.");
  30:     router.push("/login");
  31:   }
  32: 
  33:   return (
  34:     <Form
  35:       layout="vertical"
  36:       onFinish={handleSubmit}
  37:       requiredMark={false}
  38:     >
  39:       <Title level={3}>새 비밀번호 설정</Title>
  40:       <Paragraph>
  41:         새로 사용할 비밀번호를 입력하세요. 8자 이상이어야 합니다.
  42:       </Paragraph>
  43:       <Form.Item
  44:         label="새 비밀번호"
  45:         name="password"
  46:         rules={[
  47:           { required: true, message: "비밀번호를 입력하세요" },
  48:           { min: 8, message: "비밀번호는 8자 이상이어야 합니다" },
  49:         ]}
  50:       >
  51:         <Input.Password autoComplete="new-password" />
  52:       </Form.Item>
  53:       <Form.Item
  54:         label="비밀번호 확인"
  55:         name="passwordConfirm"
  56:         dependencies={["password"]}
  57:         rules={[
  58:           { required: true, message: "비밀번호를 다시 입력하세요" },
  59:           ({ getFieldValue }) => ({
  60:             validator(_, value) {
  61:               if (!value || getFieldValue("password") === value) {
  62:                 return Promise.resolve();
  63:               }
  64:               return Promise.reject(new Error("비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다"));
  65:             },
  66:           }),
  67:         ]}
  68:       >
  69:         <Input.Password autoComplete="new-password" />
  70:       </Form.Item>
  71:       <Form.Item>
  72:         <Button type="primary" htmlType="submit" block loading={submitting}>
  73:           비밀번호 변경
  74:         </Button>
  75:       </Form.Item>
  76:     </Form>
  77:   );
  78: }

 234: | 6 | `session_expired` Alert 가 안 뜸 | Supabase 쿠키 이름 변경 (`sb-*-auth-token` prefix) | `proxy.ts` 의 stale-cookie 감지 조건 갱신 |
 235: | 7 | cleanup job 이 한 번도 안 돈 듯 함 | pg_cron extension 미활성 / 마이그레이션 #23 미적용 | `select * from cron.job where jobname = 'cleanup_unconfirmed_users_daily';` |
 236: | 8 | 미인증 사용자가 아바타 업로드 성공 | `is_email_confirmed` RLS 정책 미적용 (#24 누락) | `\df private.is_email_confirmed` + `\dp storage.objects` 로 정책 확인 |

  16:       "otp_expired",
  17:       "flow_state_expired",
  18:       "flow_state_not_found",
  19:       "bad_code_verifier",
  20:       "user_not_found",
  21:       "over_email_send_rate_limit",
  22:       "over_request_rate_limit",
  23:       "email_not_confirmed",
  24:       "signup_disabled",
  25:       "access_denied",
  26:       "unknown",
  27:     ];
  28:     for (const code of canonical) {
  29:       expect(mapSupabaseErrorCode(code)).toBe(code);
  30:     }
  31:   });
  32: 
  33:   it("falls back to 'unknown' for unsupported code", () => {
  34:     expect(mapSupabaseErrorCode("some_new_supabase_code")).toBe("unknown");
  35:     expect(mapSupabaseErrorCode("")).toBe("unknown");
  36:     expect(mapSupabaseErrorCode(null)).toBe("unknown");
  37:     expect(mapSupabaseErrorCode(undefined)).toBe("unknown");
  38:   });
  39: });
  40: 
  41: describe("isValidReason", () => {
  42:   it("accepts canonical reasons", () => {
  43:     expect(isValidReason("otp_expired")).toBe(true);
  44:     expect(isValidReason("user_not_found")).toBe(true);
  45:   });
  46:   it("rejects non-canonical or empty", () => {
  47:     expect(isValidReason("invented_reason")).toBe(false);
  48:     expect(isValidReason("")).toBe(false);
  49:     expect(isValidReason(null)).toBe(false);
  50:   });
  51: });
  52: 
  53: describe("REASON_CONTENT", () => {
  54:   it("covers every AuthErrorReason exactly once", () => {
  55:     const keys: AuthErrorReason[] = [
  56:       "otp_expired",
  57:       "flow_state_expired",
  58:       "flow_state_not_found",
  59:       "bad_code_verifier",

  79:

  81:     expect(REASON_CONTENT.user_not_found.primary.kind).toBe("signup");
  82:   });
  83:

  85:     expect(REASON_CONTENT.over_email_send_rate_limit.hasCountdown).toBe(true);
  86:     expect(REASON_CONTENT.over_request_rate_limit.hasCountdown).toBe(true);
  87:   });
  88:

  90:     expect(REASON_CONTENT.otp_expired.showsEmailField).toBe(true);
  91:     expect(REASON_CONTENT.email_not_confirmed.showsEmailField).toBe(true);
  92:     expect(REASON_CONTENT.over_email_send_rate_limit.showsEmailField).toBe(true);
  93:   });
  94: });

  96: describe("sanitizeNext", () => {
  97:   it("accepts a relative path", () => {
  98:     expect(sanitizeNext("/dashboard")).toBe("/dashboard");
  99:     expect(sanitizeNext("/practice/problems")).toBe("/practice/problems");
 100:   });
 101: 
 102:   it("falls back when not starting with /", () => {
 103:     expect(sanitizeNext("dashboard")).toBe("/dashboard");
 104:   });
 105: 
 106:   it("rejects protocol-relative URL", () => {
 107:     expect(sanitizeNext("//evil.com")).toBe("/dashboard");
 108:   });
 109: 
 110:   it("rejects absolute URL", () => {
 111:     expect(sanitizeNext("https://evil.com")).toBe("/dashboard");
 112:     expect(sanitizeNext("http://evil.com/dashboard")).toBe("/dashboard");
 113:   });
 114: 
 115:   it("rejects javascript scheme", () => {
 116:     expect(sanitizeNext("javascript:alert(1)")).toBe("/dashboard");
 117:   });
 118: 
 119:   it("uses custom fallback", () => {
 120:     expect(sanitizeNext(null, "/home")).toBe("/home");
 121:     expect(sanitizeNext("//evil", "/home")).toBe("/home");
 122:   });
 123: });

 129:     );
 130:     expect(r.errorCode).toBe("otp_expired");
 131:     expect(r.errorDescription).toBe("Email link is invalid or has expired");
 132:     expect(r.accessToken).toBeNull();
 133:   });
 134: 
 135:   it("parses access_token style fragment (Supabase implicit flow success)", () => {
 136:     const r = parseAuthFragment(

 138:     );
 139:     expect(r.accessToken).toBe("abc.def.ghi");
 140:     expect(r.refreshToken).toBe("xyz123");
 141:     expect(r.tokenType).toBe("bearer");

 143:     expect(r.errorCode).toBeNull();
 144:   });

 149:   });

 153:     expect(parseAuthFragment("#").errorCode).toBeNull();

 155:     expect(parseAuthFragment(undefined).errorCode).toBeNull();

 163:     expect(sanitizeRetryAfterSeconds("1")).toBe(1);

 165:   });

 168:     expect(sanitizeRetryAfterSeconds("0")).toBeNull();
 169:     expect(sanitizeRetryAfterSeconds("-1")).toBeNull();
 170:     expect(sanitizeRetryAfterSeconds("86401")).toBeNull();

 172:

 174:     expect(sanitizeRetryAfterSeconds("60.5")).toBeNull();

 176:     expect(sanitizeRetryAfterSeconds("")).toBeNull();

codex
VERDICT: FAIL  
SUMMARY: 7개 Round 1 이슈 중 대부분은 닫혔습니다. 하지만 비밀번호 재설정 흐름이 §4.3에서는 맞게 고쳐졌는데, §7 Dashboard 설정 설명이 여전히 recovery 링크를 `/auth/callback` 경유처럼 말해 코드와 충돌합니다.

### 1. Callback structure
Verdict: PASS  
Finding: 문서가 `route.ts` + `callback-fragment/page.tsx` 구조를 정확히 가리킵니다. `docs/development/auth-overview.md:69`, `:115-122` ↔ `src/app/auth/callback/route.ts:56-130`, `src/app/auth/callback-fragment/page.tsx:25-37`. cookie silent-fail 설명도 `route.ts:3-10`, `:103-105`와 맞습니다.  
Suggested fix: 없음.

### 2. Password reset flow
Verdict: FAIL  
Finding: §4.3 자체는 맞습니다. `docs/development/auth-overview.md:109-110`은 `/auth/callback` 미경유를 명시하고, 코드는 `redirectTo: buildAuthRedirectUrl("/password-reset/confirm")`입니다(`src/components/auth/PasswordResetRequestForm.tsx:21-23`).  
하지만 §7이 다시 “confirm/recovery/magic link 의 `{{ .ConfirmationURL }}` 이 `/auth/callback?...` 형식인지 확인”이라고 해서 recovery 흐름과 충돌합니다(`docs/development/auth-overview.md:219`).  
Suggested fix: line 219에서 `recovery`를 빼거나, recovery는 `/password-reset/confirm` 직행이라고 분리하세요.

### 3. NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL env
Verdict: CONCERN  
Finding: 변수 자체는 문서와 예시에 모두 있습니다. `docs/development/auth-overview.md:212`, `.env.example:15-17`, `src/lib/auth/redirect-url.ts:29-35`.  
다만 `E10 적용 후` 문구가 최종 문서에 남아 있어 패치 흔적처럼 읽힙니다. 또 `.env.example:19`의 `ACCESS_TOKEN`이 “Browser-visible” 섹션 아래에 있어, 문서의 서버 전용 설명(`docs/development/auth-overview.md:214`)과 배치가 어색합니다.  
Suggested fix: `E10 적용 후` 삭제. `ACCESS_TOKEN`은 별도 CLI/PAT 또는 server-only 섹션으로 이동.

### 4. Rate limit + session policy
Verdict: PASS  
Finding: 문서가 Round 1 지적값으로 정정됐습니다. OTP 360/hour와 same-user 60초는 문서의 Supabase production checklist 링크와 맞고(`docs/development/auth-overview.md:179-180`), 세션 설명도 Supabase Sessions 문서와 맞습니다(`docs/development/auth-overview.md:277`). Built-in SMTP 2/hour도 Supabase SMTP 문서와 일치합니다(`docs/development/auth-overview.md:181`, `:184`).  
Suggested fix: 없음. Sources: https://supabase.com/docs/guides/deployment/going-into-prod, https://supabase.com/docs/guides/auth/sessions, https://supabase.com/docs/guides/auth/auth-smtp

### 5. PW 8-64 drift
Verdict: PASS  
Finding: drift를 사실 기록으로만 남겼습니다. IA는 8-64자(`docs/IA/01-A-01-sign-up/description.md:58-60`, `docs/IA/28-X-06-password-reset/description.md:52-54`), 구현은 `min: 8` only(`src/components/auth/SignUpForm.tsx:71-77`, `src/components/auth/PasswordResetConfirmForm.tsx:43-49`), 문서는 product 결정 후 별건 PR이라고 말합니다(`docs/development/auth-overview.md:296`).  
Suggested fix: 없음.

### 6. SSoT matrix
Verdict: PASS  
Finding: 테스트 파일 인용이 들어갔습니다. 라우트 행은 `tests/integration/route-matrix.test.ts`를 포함하고(`docs/development/auth-overview.md:289`, test 근거 `tests/integration/route-matrix.test.ts:32-43`), reason 행은 `tests/lib/auth/error-mapping.test.ts`를 포함합니다(`docs/development/auth-overview.md:290`, test 근거 `tests/lib/auth/error-mapping.test.ts:13-31`, `:53-94`). `docs/sitemap.md` 중복은 cosmetic입니다.  
Suggested fix: 없음.

### 7. Project state honesty
Verdict: PASS  
Finding: 상단 주의문이 보이고 정확합니다. `docs/development/auth-overview.md:3-8`이 현재 worktree 구현 기준이며 stale pre-implementation 표기를 경고합니다.  
Suggested fix: 없음.

### 8. Debug SQL hygiene
Verdict: PASS  
Finding: 첫 코드 블록에 `supabase db remote commit --linked`가 없습니다. 현재는 linked DB에서 `psql "$DATABASE_URL"` dry-run만 안내합니다(`docs/development/auth-overview.md:300-307`). `rg "supabase db remote commit"`도 no match였습니다.  
Suggested fix: 없음.

## Cross-cutting
Coherence: 대부분은 한 문서처럼 읽히지만, §4.3과 §7 line 219가 서로 다른 password reset 경로를 말해 “grep 패치 흔적”이 남았습니다.  
New errors: 주요 신규 오류는 line 219의 recovery/callback 충돌입니다. 보조 concern은 `.env.example`의 `ACCESS_TOKEN` 섹션 배치입니다.  
Verification: `node scripts/ai-workflow-check.mjs --repo .`는 이번 실행에서 `git status --porcelain failed`로 실패했습니다. 직접 `git status --porcelain --untracked-files=all`은 성공했습니다.
